\displaystyle({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\Big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\Big)^{-1}\big({\cal E}_{\overline{\omega}}\big)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\,.

So (
6.43
) gives that

\displaystyle{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big(({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\Big\{\Big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\Big)^{-1}\big({\cal E}_{\overline{\omega}}\big)\Big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\phi\big(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace t_{\overline{\omega}},{\cal E}_{\overline{\omega}}\big).

(6.52)

By (R2), there exists

for any

. Given
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\{s\negthinspace<\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace<\negthinspace r\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{s,r,{\cal E}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{*}
,
since

is a Brownian motion with respect to the filration

under

by (

we can deduce from (

\displaystyle\hskip-19.91684ptE_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}-{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})}\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\Big]({\overline{\omega}})

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace{\overline{P}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}\big(({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}\big(({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}\Big\{\Big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\Big)^{-1}\big({\cal E}_{\overline{\omega}}\big)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\Big\}

(6.53)

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace{\overline{P}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}\Big\{\Big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\Big)^{-1}\big({\cal E}_{\overline{\omega}}\big)\Big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\phi\big(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace t_{\overline{\omega}},{\cal E}_{\overline{\omega}}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big(({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\big).

Since
\{s\negthinspace<\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace<\negthinspace r\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}

.
Then we see from (

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big(\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big(({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\big){\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E}){\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big(\{s\negthinspace<\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace<\negthinspace r\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+{\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace r\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\big)

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E}){\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\big).

Since
{\overline{W}}^{t}
is a Brownian motion under
{\overline{P}}

and since
{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{s}
,

\displaystyle{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big(\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E})\Big({\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\Big)=\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E}).

II.c.2)
Let

.

\displaystyle{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\Big(\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E}){\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}({\overline{A}}),\quad\,\forall\,{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{s}\,.

(6.54)

Let
{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{s}
. Since
{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\overline{\gamma}}

one can derive from (

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\Big(\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\Big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\Big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{A}}\cap\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\}}{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big(({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})\big){\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\Big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\Big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\Big({\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\geq r\}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{A}}\cap\{s<{\overline{\gamma}}<r\}\}}E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}-{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})}\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\Big]({\overline{\omega}}){\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\Big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\Big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\Big({\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\geq r\}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\cap\{s<{\overline{\gamma}}<r\}\}}{\bf 1}_{({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}-{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s})^{-1}({\cal E})}\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\Big]\bigg]

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\Big(\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big(\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E}){\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\big),

(6.55)

where we used the independence of

in the fifth equality above.
This equality directly verifies (

(6.56)

By (

We set

\displaystyle\hskip-11.38092pt\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{A}}_{X}\cap{\overline{A}}^{\overline{\gamma}}_{k}\}}{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{k}\big)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\Big\{\Big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{s}\Big)^{-1}({\cal E})\Big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}_{X}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}^{\overline{\gamma}}_{k}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{k}\big)\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E})\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}_{X}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}_{m}\big)\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E}),

So the Lambda-system
\overline{\Lambda}^{t}_{s,r}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\Big\{{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{B}\big({\overline{\Omega}}\big)\negthinspace:{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big(\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\big)^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\phi(r\negthinspace-\negthinspace s,{\cal E}){\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}\big)\Big\}

contains the Pi-system

\Big\{\Big(\underset{i=1}{\overset{n}{\cap}}{\overline{X}}^{-1}_{t_{i}}({\cal E}^{o}_{i})\Big)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\Big(\underset{j=1}{\overset{m}{\cap}}\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{s_{j}},\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s_{j}},{\overline{X}}_{s_{j}}\big)^{-1}({\cal E}_{j})\Big)\negthinspace:0\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace t_{1}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\cdots\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace t_{n}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace t\negthinspace=\negthinspace s_{1}\negthinspace<\negthinspace s_{2}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\cdots\negthinspace<\negthinspace s_{m-1}\negthinspace<\negthinspace s_{m}\negthinspace=\negthinspace s,\,\{{\cal E}^{o}_{i}\}^{n}_{i=1}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R}^{l}),\,\{{\cal E}_{j}\}^{m}_{j=1}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R}^{d+1+l})\Big\}
,
which generates
{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{s}
.
In light of Dynkin’s Pi-Lambda Theorem, we obtain
{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\overline{\Lambda}^{t}_{s,r}
, proving (
6.54
).

Hence,
{\overline{W}}^{t}
is a Brownian motion with respect to the filtration
{\overline{{\bf G}}}^{t}
under
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
.
Namely,
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
satisfies (D2) of
{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}
.

II.d)
In this part, we demonstrate that
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
satisfies (D1) of
{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}
.

II.d.1)
For any
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
, there is a
[0,1]-
valued
{\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{s}-
measurable random variable
\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}
on
\Omega_{0}

such that

\displaystyle\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{s}\Big]({\overline{\omega}}),\quad\,\forall\,{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}.

Since
{\overline{W}}^{t}
is a Brownian motion with respect to the filtration
{\overline{{\bf G}}}^{t}
under
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
by Part (II.c),
applying Lemma
A.1
with
t_{0}\negthinspace=\negthinspace t
,
(\Omega_{1},{\cal F}_{1},P_{1},B^{1})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\overline{\Omega}},\mathscr{B}({\overline{\Omega}}),{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon},{\overline{W}}\big)
,
(\Omega_{2},{\cal F}_{2},P_{2},B^{2})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big(\Omega_{0},\mathscr{B}(\Omega_{0}),P_{0},W\big)
and
\Phi\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{W}}
yields that

\big\{\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
is an
{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}-
adapted process and that

E_{P_{0}}\big[\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\,\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\big]
is right-continuous in
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
.
As
{\bf F}^{W^{t},P_{0}}
is a right-continuous complete filtration,
the process
\{\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
admits a càdlàg modification
\big\{\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
,
which is a
[0,1]-
valued
{\bf F}^{W^{t},P_{0}}-
adapted process.

As
{\overline{W}}^{t}
is a Brownian motion both under
{\overline{P}}

and under
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
, taking
t_{0}\negthinspace=\negthinspace t
,
(\Omega_{1},{\cal F}_{1},P_{1},B^{1})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\overline{\Omega}},\mathscr{B}({\overline{\Omega}}),{\overline{P}}\hbox{ or }{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon},{\overline{W}}\big)
,
(\Omega_{2},{\cal F}_{2},P_{2},B^{2})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big(\Omega_{0},\mathscr{B}(\Omega_{0}),P_{0},W\big)
and
\Phi\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{W}}
in Lemma
A.1
renders that

the process
\big\{\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
is both

{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t},{\overline{P}}}-
adapted and
{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t},{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}-
adapted

\displaystyle\hskip-96.73918pt\hbox{as well as that }\;{\overline{W}}^{-1}({\cal N})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty})\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}),~\;\,\forall\,{\cal N}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{P_{0}}\big({\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{\infty}\big).

(6.59)

Given
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
,
since
\big\{\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\negthinspace\neq\negthinspace\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{P_{0}}\big({\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{\infty}\big)
,
(
6.59
) shows that

\widehat{{\cal N}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace\neq\negthinspace\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{-1}\Big(\big\{\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\negthinspace\neq\negthinspace\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big\}\Big)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty})
.
Applying Lemma
A.6
with
\big({\overline{P}},{\overline{{\cal W}}},\mathfrak{F}_{\cdot},{\overline{\xi}}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon},\{{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}\}_{s\in[t,\infty)},{\overline{{\bf G}}}^{t},\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\big)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{s}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\Big],\quad\hbox{${\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}-$a.s.}

(6.60)

Let
{\overline{\zeta}}
be a
[t,\infty)-
valued
{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}-
stopping time, let
{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\zeta}}
and let
n\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
.
We set
s^{n}_{i}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace t\negthinspace+\negthinspace i2^{-n}
,
\,\forall\,i\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\{0\}

and define
{\overline{\zeta}}_{n}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\sum_{i\in\mathbb{N}}s^{n}_{i}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\zeta}}\in[s^{n}_{i-1},s^{n}_{i})\}}
.
For any
i\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
, one can deduce from (
6.60
) and the monotone convergence theorem that

\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\zeta}}_{n}}\negthinspace({\overline{W}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\sum_{i\in\mathbb{N}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\zeta}}\in[s^{n}_{i-1},s^{n}_{i})\}}\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s^{n}_{i}}\big({\overline{W}}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\sum_{i\in\mathbb{N}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\zeta}}\in[s^{n}_{i-1},s^{n}_{i})\}}E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s^{n}_{i}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\sum_{i\in\mathbb{N}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\zeta}}\in[s^{n}_{i-1},s^{n}_{i})\}}\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s^{n}_{i}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\zeta}}_{n}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\Big]
,
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}-
a.s.
As
{\overline{\zeta}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{n\to\infty}{\lim}\negthinspace\mathop{\downarrow}\,{\overline{\zeta}}_{n}
, the right-continuity of process
\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}
,
\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}

and the bounded convergence theorem imply that

\displaystyle\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{\overline{\zeta}}\big({\overline{W}}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{n\to\infty}{\lim}\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\zeta}}_{n}}\negthinspace({\overline{W}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{n\to\infty}{\lim}E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\zeta}}_{n}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{\overline{\zeta}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\Big],\quad\hbox{${\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}-$a.s.}

(6.61)

As the right-continuous
{\bf F}^{W^{t},P_{0}}-
adapted process
\big\{\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
is
{\bf F}^{W^{t},P_{0}}-
optional,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathscr{U}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace e^{s}\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\mathop{\underline{\rm lim}}}\frac{1}{\delta}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{(s-\delta)\vee t}(\omega_{0})\Big),\quad\,\forall\,(s,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}

is a
[-\infty,\infty]-
valued,
{\bf F}^{W^{t},P_{0}}-
optional process.
Similar to Lemma 2.4 of
[
38
]
,
we can construct a
[-\infty,\infty]-
valued
{\bf F}^{W^{t}}-
predictable process

\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
with

\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\widehat{\mathscr{U}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})
for
ds\negthinspace\times\negthinspace dP_{0}-
a.s.
(s,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
.
Using Fubini Theorem, one can find a
{\cal N}^{\varepsilon}_{\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{P_{0}}\big({\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{\infty}\big)
such that
for any
\omega_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\cal N}^{\varepsilon}_{\mathscr{U}}\big)^{c}
,

\displaystyle\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\widehat{\mathscr{U}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\quad\hbox{for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty).

(6.62)

By (
6.59
),
{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{-1}\big({\cal N}^{\varepsilon}_{\mathscr{U}}\big)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)
. We define a
\mathbb{U}-
valued
{\bf F}^{W^{t}}-
predictable process by

\displaystyle\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\mathscr{I}^{-1}\big(\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\big){\bf 1}_{\{\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\in\mathfrak{E}\}}\negthinspace+\negthinspace u_{0}{\bf 1}_{\{\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}(\omega_{0})\notin\mathfrak{E}\}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{U},\quad\,\forall\,(s,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}.

II.d.2)

Let
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
and let
{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}
.
Since
\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\overline{\gamma}}

and
{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\overline{\gamma}}
,
(
6.45
) and (
6.36
) show that

E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{A}}\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}}\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\Big]\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{\omega}}){\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}\,\big){\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}}{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big]\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{\omega}}){\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}\,\big){\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{A}}{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big]
.
Letting
{\overline{A}}
varies over
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}
and taking
{\overline{\zeta}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace s
in (
6.61
), we obtain

{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{W}})
,
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}-
a.s.
By the right-continuity of processes
{\overline{J}}^{\,t}
and
\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}
,
it holds for all
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
except on
a
{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{1}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)
that

\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})
,
\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big[t,{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\big]
.

Let
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{1}\big)^{c}
. Since the Lebesgue differentiation theorem yields that

\displaystyle\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\frac{1}{\delta}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{(s-\delta)\vee t}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\frac{1}{\delta}\Big({\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{(s-\delta)\vee t}({\overline{\omega}})\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\,\frac{1}{\delta}\int_{(s-\delta)\vee t}^{s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\negthinspace=\negthinspace e^{-s}\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\big)

for a.e.
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big(t,{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)
, we see from (
6.62
) that

\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\widehat{\mathscr{U}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace e^{s}\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\,\frac{1}{\delta}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{(s-\delta)\vee t}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\big)
for a.e.
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big(t,{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)
. Thus,

\displaystyle\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{1}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\overline{\mu}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\hbox{ for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(t,{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}))\big\}\negthinspace\supset\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{1}\big)^{c}.

(6.63)

As
\big\{\int_{t}^{s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\mu^{\varepsilon}_{r}\big)dr\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}

is an
{\bf F}^{W^{t}}-
adapted process,
Lemma
A.1
(1) shows that

\big\{\int_{t}^{s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\mu^{\varepsilon}_{r}({\overline{W}})\big)dr\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}

is an
{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}-
adapted continuous process, which together with the
{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}-
adaptedness of continuous process
{\overline{J}}^{\,t}
implies

\displaystyle\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{1}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\Big\{\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\mu^{\varepsilon}_{r}\big({\overline{W}}\big)\big)dr\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s},\;\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\Big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{s\in\mathbb{Q}\cap[t,\infty)}{\overset{}{\cap}}\Big\{\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\mu^{\varepsilon}_{r}\big({\overline{W}}\big)\big)dr\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\Big\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\overline{\gamma}}\,.\quad

(6.64)

Then we can derive from (
6.48
) and (
6.63
) that

1\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big(({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{1}\big)^{c}\big)\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big(\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{1}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big(\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{1}\big)
.
Similar to (
6.64
),

\displaystyle{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{1}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{U}}_{\negthinspace s}({\overline{\omega}})\hbox{ for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(t,{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}))\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{s\in\mathbb{Q}\cap[t,\infty)}{\overset{}{\cap}}\Big\{\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\mu^{\varepsilon}_{r}({\overline{W}})\big)dr\Big\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\overline{\gamma}}\,.

Applying (
6.48
) again renders that

\displaystyle{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{1}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{1}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{\Omega}}_{\mu}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{1}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{\Omega}}_{\mu}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{1}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big(\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{1}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1.

(6.65)

II.d.3)

Let
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
and let
{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}
.
Since

E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}}\big({\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}}\big({\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\big]

by (
6.36
), letting
{\overline{A}}
varies over
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}
and using (
6.61
) again yield that

\displaystyle\quad{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}}\big({\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}}E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}}\big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{W}})\big),\quad\hbox{${\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}-$a.s.}\quad

The right-continuity of processes
{\overline{J}}^{\,t}
and
\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}
assures an
{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{2}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)

such that for any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{2}\big)^{c}
and any
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),\infty)
,
one has
\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{\omega}}\big)
.

Let
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{2}\big)^{c}
.
Since the Lebesgue differentiation theorem implies that

\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\frac{1}{\delta}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace\delta)\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\frac{1}{\delta}\Big({\overline{J}}^{\,t}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,t}\big((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace\delta)\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{\omega}}\big)\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\,\frac{1}{\delta}\int_{(s-\delta)\vee{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})}^{s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\negthinspace=\negthinspace e^{-s}\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\big)
for a.e.
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),\infty\big)
,
(
6.62
) shows that

\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\widehat{\mathscr{U}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace e^{s}\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\,\frac{1}{\delta}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace\delta)\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\big)
for a.e.
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),\infty\big)
. So
\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\overline{\mu}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\hbox{ for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),\infty\big)\big\}\negthinspace\supset\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{2}\big)^{c}
. Set

\displaystyle{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{U}}_{\negthinspace s}({\overline{\omega}})\hbox{ for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),\infty\big)\big\}.

(6.66)

As
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{2}\big)^{c}\big)\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\big)
, we obtain that

\displaystyle{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{\Omega}}_{\mu}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{\Omega}}_{\mu}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\big).

(6.67)

II.d.4)

We denote
\mathbb{Q}_{t}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\{t+q\negthinspace:q\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{Q}_{+}\}
and set
{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\Upsilon}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\underset{s\in\mathbb{Q}_{t}}{\overset{}{\cup}}\big\{\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace\neq\negthinspace\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\big\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)
.

Fix
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{Q}_{t}
. Given
k\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
, we set
s^{k}_{i}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace t\negthinspace+\negthinspace i2^{-k}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t)
for
i\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0,1,\cdots,2^{k}
.
Since
\big\{\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{r}({\overline{W}})\big\}_{r\in[t,\infty)}
is a
[0,1]-
valued
{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}-
adapted process,
the random variable

{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon,k}_{s}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}\geq s\}}\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace+\negthinspace\sum^{2^{k}}_{i=1}{\bf 1}_{\{s^{k}_{i-1}\leq{\overline{\gamma}}<s^{k}_{i}\}}\Upsilon^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{0.45pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{s^{k}_{i}}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,1]
is
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{s}-
measurable.
Then
{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\underset{k\to\infty}{\mathop{\underline{\rm lim}}}{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon,k}_{s}
is also a
[0,1]-
valued
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{s}-
measurable random variable.

For any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\Upsilon}\big)^{c}
, the right-continuity of process
\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}
shows that

\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\land\negthinspace s,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{k\to\infty}{\lim}\Big({\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\geq s\}}\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\sum^{2^{k}}_{i=1}{\bf 1}_{\{s^{k}_{i-1}\leq{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})<s^{k}_{i}\}}\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s^{k}_{i}}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{k\to\infty}{\lim}{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon,k}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})
and thus

\displaystyle\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\land\negthinspace s,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}).

(6.68)

As
{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\Upsilon}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)
, there exists
{\overline{A}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}
such that

{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\Upsilon}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\overline{A}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}
and
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
, which implies

0\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\big){\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
.
Since the random variable
{\overline{\Omega}}\ni{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\mapsto\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\big)
is
\sigma\Big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\infty}\big)\Big)-
measurable by (
6.41
),
there exists
{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\infty}\big)

such that for any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\big)^{c}

\displaystyle{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{A}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0\quad\hbox{and thus}\quad{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\Upsilon}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big).

(6.69)

Let
\big\{\overline{O}_{j}\big\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}}
be a countable Pi-system that generates
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{s}
. Let
j\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
and
{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}
.
One can deduce from (

), (

) and (

\displaystyle\hskip-34.14322pt\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{\Omega}}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\Big(\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\Big)\Big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\cap\overline{O}_{j}}\Big(\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\Big)\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\cap\overline{O}_{j}}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{W}})\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\cap\overline{O}_{j}}E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\Big]\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\cap\overline{O}_{j}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\Big]\Big]

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\cap\overline{O}_{j}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{\Omega}}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\,.

So the Lambda-system
\overline{\Lambda}^{\varepsilon}_{s,j}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\Big\{{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{B}_{\overline{P}}({\overline{\Omega}})\negthinspace:\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{\Omega}}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\big(\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big)\big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{\Omega}}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\\
\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\Big\}

contains
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}
and
\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big(\mathscr{B}({\overline{\Omega}})\big)
.
As

is closed under intersection,
we know from Dynkin’s Pi-Lambda Theorem that

or

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{\Omega}}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\Big(\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\Big)\Big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{\Omega}}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{A}}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}}),\quad\,\forall\,{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\sigma\Big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big(\mathscr{B}\big({\overline{\Omega}}\big)\big)\Big).

(6.70)

Taking
\phi\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\big(\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big)

and

are two
[-1,1]-

\sigma\Big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\infty}\big)\Big)-

\displaystyle{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{*}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\Big(\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\Big)\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{*}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\Big].

(6.71)

Set
{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\underset{j\in\mathbb{N}}{\overset{}{\cup}}{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{s,j}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\infty}\big)

Since it holds for any

for any

\displaystyle E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{W}})\Big)\Big]\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\Big(\Upsilon^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{\xi}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\Big)\Big]\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\big(\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\overline{\Upsilon}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}\big)\Big]\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}\overline{\Upsilon}^{\overline{\omega}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}\vee s}\Big]\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\overline{O}_{j}}{\overline{J}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}\vee s}\Big].

Then Dynkin’s Pi-Lambda Theorem implies that the Lambda-system
\Big\{{\cal E}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{B}_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\overline{\Omega}}\big)\negthinspace:E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\cal E}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{W}})\Big)\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\cal E}{\overline{J}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}\vee s}\Big]\Big\}

:

\displaystyle E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\cal E}}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{W}})\Big)\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\cal E}}{\overline{J}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}\vee s}\Big],\quad\,\forall\,{\cal E}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t},{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}_{s}.

(6.72)

If
{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace s
, then
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}\vee s}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{\emptyset,{\overline{\Omega}}\}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{s}
; if

.
In both cases, we see that
{\overline{J}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}\vee s}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}\vee s}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{s}
.
Since
\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}({\overline{W}})
is
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t},{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}_{s}-
measurable by (

) and (

),
