{\cal E}
run through
{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t},{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}_{s}
in (
6.72
),
we can find some
{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)
such that

\displaystyle\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\land\negthinspace s,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{J}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}\vee s}\big({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big),\quad\,\forall\,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big({\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\big)^{c}.

(6.73)

Now, set
{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\underset{s\in\mathbb{Q}_{t}}{\overset{}{\cup}}{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{s\in\mathbb{Q}_{t}}{\overset{}{\cup}}\underset{j\in\mathbb{N}}{\overset{}{\cup}}{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{s,j}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\infty}\big)
and fix
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp}\big)^{c}
.
We also set
{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\underset{s\in\mathbb{Q}_{t}}{\overset{}{\cup}}{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)

and let
{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp,{\overline{\omega}}}\big)^{c}
.
The right-continuity of process
\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}
, (
5.1
) and (
6.73
) render that

\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big(t_{\overline{\omega}},{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\int_{t_{\overline{\omega}}}^{s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}_{r}\big({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)\big)dr
,
\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t_{\overline{\omega}},\infty)
.
By the Lebesgue differentiation theorem, we have

\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\frac{1}{\delta}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace\delta)\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace t_{\overline{\omega}},{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\,\frac{1}{\delta}\int_{(s-\delta)\vee t_{\overline{\omega}}}^{s}e^{-r}\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}_{r}\big({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)\big)dr\negthinspace=\negthinspace e^{-s}\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}_{s}\big({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)\big)
for a.e.
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(t_{\overline{\omega}},\infty)

and thus

\mathscr{U}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\widehat{\mathscr{U}}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace e^{s}\underset{\delta\to 0+}{\lim}\,\frac{1}{\delta}\Big(\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\widehat{\Upsilon}^{\varepsilon}\big((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace\delta)\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace t_{\overline{\omega}},{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\mathscr{I}\big(\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}_{s}\big({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)\big)
for a.e.
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(t_{\overline{\omega}},\infty)
.
It follows that
\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\hbox{ for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(t_{\overline{\omega}},\infty)\big\}\negthinspace\supset\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp,{\overline{\omega}}}\big)^{c}
.
Then (
6.43
) and (
6.69
) show that

1\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\Big\{{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,\varepsilon}_{\negthinspace\mathscr{U}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp,{\overline{\omega}}}\big)^{c}\Big\}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big(\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2,{\overline{\omega}}}\big)
.
Since
{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\mu}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\mu}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{U}}_{\negthinspace s}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\hbox{ for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big(t_{\overline{\omega}},\infty\big)\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\mu}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2,{\overline{\omega}}}
by (
6.66
), we further see that

\displaystyle{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big(\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2,{\overline{\omega}}}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1,\quad\,\forall\,{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp}\big)^{c},

(6.74)

which together with (
6.65
) and (
6.67
) yields that

\displaystyle{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big\{{\overline{U}}_{s}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}}),\hbox{ for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(t,\infty)\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{1}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{\varepsilon}_{2}\big){\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1.

Hence,
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
satisfies (D1) of
{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}
. We set
\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\mu^{\varepsilon}_{s}({\overline{W}})
,
\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
.

II.e)

We next show that
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
satisfies (D2
′
) of
{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}
and thus satisfies (D3) of
{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}
.

II.e.1)
Set
{\overline{\Omega}}_{X}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{X}}_{s}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf x}(s),\,\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,t]\big\}
.
We know from the proof of Proposition
5.1
that
{\overline{\Omega}}^{c}_{X}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}_{\negthinspace X}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:{\overline{X}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\neq\negthinspace\overline{\mathscr{X}}^{t,{\bf x},\overline{\mu}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\hbox{ for some }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)\big\}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}_{1}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}_{*}
.
Given
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}_{X}
, one has
{\overline{\Omega}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:{\overline{X}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{X}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}),\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,t]\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}_{X}
and (
6.43
) implies that
{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{\Omega}}_{X}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1
.
As
{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{\Omega}}_{X}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1
by (D2
′
) of Remark
3.1
, one can deduce that

{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big({\overline{\Omega}}_{X}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}_{X}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{*}}1\negthinspace\cdot\negthinspace{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{P}}({\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*})\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1
.
Applying Proposition
1.2
with
(\Omega,{\cal F},P,B,X,\mu)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\overline{\Omega}},\mathscr{B}({\overline{\Omega}}),{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon},{\overline{W}},{\overline{X}},\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}\big)
renders that

\big\{{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{s\land{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}}(\varphi)\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}

is a bounded
{\overline{{\bf F}}}^{t}-
adapted process under
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
.

II.e.2)

Fix
(\varphi,n)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathfrak{C}(\mathbb{R}^{d+l})\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
and let
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp}\big)^{c}
.
We define a
C^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d+l})
function

\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}(w,x)\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\varphi\big(w\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),x\big)
,
(w,x)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{R}^{d+l}

and define an
{\overline{{\bf F}}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}-
stopping time
{\overline{\zeta}}^{n}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\inf\big\{s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t_{\overline{\omega}},\infty)\negthinspace:\big|({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{s},{\overline{X}}_{s})({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\mathfrak{a}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\big|\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace n\big\}
,
{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}

with
\mathfrak{a}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big(\negthinspace\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\bf 0}\big)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{R}^{d+l}
.
For
i\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0,1,2
and
{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2,{\overline{\omega}}}
, since
D^{i}\varphi\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),{\overline{X}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace D^{i}\varphi\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{X}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace D^{i}\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),{\overline{X}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)
,
\,\forall\,r\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t_{\overline{\omega}},\infty)
,
we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-34.14322pt\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{r_{2}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{r_{1}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\negthinspace=\negthinspace-\negthinspace\int_{r_{1}}^{r_{2}}\negthinspace\overline{b}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace\cdot\negthinspace D\varphi\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{\negthinspace r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),{\overline{X}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)dr\negthinspace-\negthinspace\frac{1}{2}\negthinspace\int_{r_{1}}^{r_{2}}\negthinspace\overline{\sigma}\,\overline{\sigma}^{T}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace:\negthinspace D^{2}\varphi\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{\negthinspace r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),{\overline{X}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)dr

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\negthinspace=\negthinspace-\negthinspace\int_{r_{1}}^{r_{2}}\negthinspace\overline{b}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace\cdot\negthinspace D\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{\negthinspace r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),{\overline{X}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)dr\negthinspace-\negthinspace\frac{1}{2}\negthinspace\int_{r_{1}}^{r_{2}}\negthinspace\overline{\sigma}\,\overline{\sigma}^{T}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace:\negthinspace D^{2}\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{\negthinspace r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),{\overline{X}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)dr

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\negthinspace=\negthinspace\Big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}}_{r_{2}}(\varphi_{\overline{\omega}})\Big)({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\Big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}}_{r_{1}}(\varphi_{\overline{\omega}})\Big)({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),\quad\,\forall\,t_{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace r_{1}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace r_{2}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty.

(6.75)

Let
t\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\negthinspace<\negthinspace r\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty
,
\{(s_{i},{\cal E}_{i})\}^{k}_{i=1}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace[t,s]\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R}^{d+l})

and set
{\overline{A}}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\underset{i=1}{\overset{k}{\cap}}({\overline{W}}^{t}_{s_{i}},{\overline{X}}_{s_{i}})^{-1}({\cal E}_{i})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal F}}}^{t}_{s}
.
If
t\negthinspace+\negthinspace n\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s
, one directly has

E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
since
{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace t\negthinspace+\negthinspace n\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s
.
So we only need to verify
E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
for the case “
t\negthinspace+\negthinspace n\negthinspace>\negthinspace s
”.

(i)
Let
t\negthinspace+\negthinspace n\negthinspace>\negthinspace s
. We first show that

\displaystyle E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}}\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0.

(6.76)

For any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{1}
and any
t\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace r_{1}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace r_{2}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})
, we have

\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{r_{2}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{r_{1}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{r_{2}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{r_{1}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}})
. So
{\bf 1}_{\widehat{A}^{\varepsilon}_{1}}\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}(\varphi)\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\widehat{A}^{\varepsilon}_{1}}\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}(\varphi)\big)

and (
6.65
) renders that

E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]
.
Since
\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace>\negthinspace s\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\Big(\underset{i=1}{\overset{k}{\cap}}({\overline{W}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s_{i}},{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land s_{i}})^{-1}({\cal E}_{i})\Big)\negthinspace\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal F}}}^{t}_{\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}
and

{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}(\varphi)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal F}}}^{t}_{\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}
, using (
6.48
) and applying (
3.2
) with

(\mathfrak{a},{\overline{\zeta}}_{1},{\overline{\zeta}}_{2})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\bf 0},{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace s,{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace r\big)

yield that

\displaystyle E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0.\qquad

(6.77)

It also holds for any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}
and any
{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace r_{1}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace r_{2}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty
that

\displaystyle\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{r_{2}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{r_{1}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{r_{2}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{r_{1}}(\varphi)\big)({\overline{\omega}}).

(6.78)

As
{\overline{P}}\Big({\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap\big(\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}\big)^{c}\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
by (
6.67
), taking
\big(\mathfrak{a},{\overline{\zeta}}_{1},{\overline{\zeta}}_{2}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\bf 0},{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace r\big)
in (
3.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274ptE_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}}\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land({\overline{\gamma}}\vee r)}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}}\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land({\overline{\gamma}}\vee r)}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2}}\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land({\overline{\gamma}}\vee r)}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}}\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land({\overline{\gamma}}\vee r)}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0.

It follows from (

\displaystyle\quad E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}\land r}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}>s\}\cap{\overline{A}}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\negthinspace\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})>s\}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{A}}\,\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\negthinspace\big[\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land({\overline{\gamma}}\vee r)}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land{\overline{\gamma}}}(\varphi)\big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}}).\quad\;\;

(6.79)

Fix

\displaystyle 0\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\overline{M}}^{\raisebox{2.5pt}{\scriptsize\hbox{$t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}$}}}_{{\overline{\zeta}}^{n}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\land(t_{\overline{\omega}}+n)\land\mathfrak{r}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\land(t+n)}(\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{\raisebox{2.5pt}{\scriptsize\hbox{$t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}$}}}_{{\overline{\zeta}}^{n}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\land(t_{\overline{\omega}}+n)\land t_{\overline{\omega}}}(\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}})\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[{\overline{M}}^{\raisebox{2.5pt}{\scriptsize\hbox{$t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}$}}}_{{\overline{\zeta}}^{n}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\land\mathfrak{r}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\land(t+n)}(\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}}_{t_{\overline{\omega}}}(\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}})\Big].

(6.80)

Because

,

{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Theta}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\widehat{A}^{\phantom{\rule[-2.27621pt]{1.0pt}{7.39772pt}}\varepsilon}_{2,{\overline{\omega}}}

\displaystyle\;\;\;\inf\negthinspace\big\{s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace:|({\overline{W}}^{t}_{s},{\overline{X}}_{s})({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})|\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace n\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\inf\big\{s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t_{\overline{\omega}},\infty)\negthinspace:\big|\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),{\overline{X}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\bf 0}\big)\big|\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace n\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{\zeta}}^{n}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}).

\displaystyle\hskip-34.14322pt\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}\negthinspace(\varphi)\big)\big({\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\land\negthinspace({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace r),{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}\negthinspace(\varphi)\big)\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}),{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big(\,{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}\negthinspace(\varphi)\big)\big({\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\land\negthinspace({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace r),{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}\negthinspace(\varphi)\big)\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)

\displaystyle\quad\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\overline{M}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}}(\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}})\big)\big({\overline{\zeta}}^{n}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\land\negthinspace(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace n)\negthinspace\land\negthinspace\mathfrak{r}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}},{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\big({\overline{M}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}}(\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}})\big)(t_{\overline{\omega}},{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}).\quad

(ii)

Define

renders that

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\Big({\overline{M}}^{\raisebox{2.5pt}{\scriptsize\hbox{$t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}$}}}_{{\overline{\zeta}}^{n}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\land(t_{\overline{\omega}}+n)\land(t+n)\land r)}(\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{\raisebox{2.5pt}{\scriptsize\hbox{$t_{\overline{\omega}},\overline{\mu}^{\overline{\omega}}$}}}_{{\overline{\zeta}}^{n}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\land(t_{\overline{\omega}}+n)\land s}(\varphi_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}})\Big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{i}}\Big]

(6.84)

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace

(6.85)

\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{s_{j}},{\overline{X}}_{s_{j}}\big)({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal E}_{j}
if and only if

\big({\overline{W}}^{t_{\overline{\omega}}}_{s_{j}},{\overline{K}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{s_{j}}\big)({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{s_{j}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\big),\overline{\mathscr{X}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{s_{j}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big({\overline{W}}^{t}_{s_{j}},{\overline{X}}_{s_{j}}\big)({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{a}_{\overset{}{{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal E}_{j,{\overline{\omega}}}
.
By (
6.82
), one has

{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{\negthinspace X}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{\negthinspace K}\big)^{c}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{A}}_{i}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{A}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{i}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{\negthinspace X}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,{\overline{\omega}}}_{\negthinspace K}\big)^{c}
.
Then we can deduce from (
6.83
), (
6.43
), (
6.45
) and (
6.85
) that

\displaystyle\hskip-34.14322ptE_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}>{\overline{\gamma}}\}}\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}>{\overline{\gamma}}\}}\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}_{i}\cap{\overline{A}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{i}}\big]

\displaystyle\hskip-8.5359pt\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{A}}_{i}\}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}({\overline{\omega}})>{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{i}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0,\quad\,\forall\,{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\{s_{i}\negthinspace<\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s_{i+1}\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{U}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{\mathfrak{N}}}^{\varepsilon}_{\sharp}\big)^{c},

and thus

\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{s_{i}<{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\leq s_{i+1}\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
.
Taking summation from
i\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1
through
i\negthinspace=\negthinspace k\negthinspace-\negthinspace 1
, we obtain from (
6.81
) that

E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}\leq s\}}\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}\cap\{{\overline{\gamma}}\leq s\}}\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{\overline{A}}\big]\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\leq s\}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
.
Adding it to (
6.76
) yields that
the Lambda-system
\Big\{{\overline{A}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{B}({\overline{\Omega}})\negthinspace:E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\big({\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land r}(\varphi)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}\land s}(\varphi)\big){\bf 1}_{{\overline{A}}}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0\Big\}

contains the Pi-system
\Big\{\underset{i=1}{\overset{k}{\cap}}({\overline{W}}^{t}_{s_{i}},{\overline{X}}_{s_{i}})^{-1}({\cal E}_{i})\negthinspace:\{(s_{i},{\cal E}_{i})\}^{k}_{i=1}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace[t,s]\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R}^{d+l})\Big\}
and thus includes
{\overline{{\cal F}}}^{t}_{s}
thanks to Dynkin’s Pi-Lambda Theorem.
Hence,
\big\{{\overline{M}}^{t,\overline{\mu}^{\varepsilon}}_{s\land{\overline{\tau}}^{t}_{n}}(\varphi)\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
is a bounded
\big({\overline{{\bf F}}}^{t},{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big)-
martingale.
According to Remark
3.1
(ii),
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
satisfies (D3) of
{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}
.

Similar to Part (II.d) in the proof of
[
4
, Theorem 5.1]
, we can construct a
[t,\infty]-
valued
{\bf F}^{W^{t},P_{0}}-
stopping time
\widehat{\tau}_{\varepsilon}
with
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\big\{{\overline{T}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\widehat{\tau}_{\varepsilon}({\overline{W}})\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1
, So

also satisfies (D4) of
{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}
.

II.f)
Fix
i\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
. Since
\big\{\int_{t}^{s}g_{i}\big(r,\overline{\mathscr{X}}^{t,{\bf x},\overline{\mu}}_{r\land\cdot},\overline{\mu}_{r}\big)dr\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}

and
\big\{\int_{t}^{s}h_{i}\big(r,\overline{\mathscr{X}}^{t,{\bf x},\overline{\mu}}_{r\land\cdot},\overline{\mu}_{r}\big)dr\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
are two

{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t},{\overline{P}}}-
adapted continuous processes,
Lemma 2.4 of
[
38
]
assures two
{\bf F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}-
predictable processes
\big\{\overline{\Phi}^{i}_{s}\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
and

\big\{\overline{\Psi}^{i}_{s}\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}

such that
{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,i,1}_{g,h}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\Big\{{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:\overline{\Phi}^{i}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\neq\negthinspace\int_{t}^{s}g_{i}\big(r,\overline{\mathscr{X}}^{t,{\bf x},\overline{\mu}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),\overline{\mu}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\hbox{ or }\overline{\Psi}^{i}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\neq\negthinspace\int_{t}^{s}h_{i}\big(r,\overline{\mathscr{X}}^{t,{\bf x},\overline{\mu}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),\overline{\mu}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\hbox{ for some }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\Big\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\infty}\big)
.
By Remark
3.2
(1),

E_{\overline{P}}\big[\int_{t}^{\infty}\negthinspace g^{-}_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace h^{-}_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\big]\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty
.
So it holds for any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
except on some
{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,i,2}_{g,h}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big(\mathscr{B}({\overline{\Omega}})\big)
that
\int_{t}^{\infty}\negthinspace g^{-}_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{U}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace h^{-}_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{U}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty
.

For any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{\negthinspace X}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}_{\mu}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big({\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,i,1}_{g,h}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{\,i,2}_{g,h}\big)^{c}
,
as
{\overline{\Omega}}^{t}_{{\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:{\overline{X}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{X}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}),\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big[0,{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\big];{\overline{U}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{U}}_{s}({\overline{\omega}})\hbox{ for a.e. }s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(t,{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}))\big\}
,
(
6.43
) and (
6.42
) show that

\displaystyle\hskip-34.14322ptE_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\int_{t}^{\overline{T}}\negthinspace g_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})}\negthinspace g_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{U}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\int_{{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})}^{\overline{T}}\negthinspace g_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\Big]

(6.86)

\displaystyle\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})}\negthinspace g_{i}\big(r,\overline{\mathscr{X}}^{t,{\bf x},\overline{\mu}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),\overline{\mu}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace\big({\overline{Y}}^{i}_{\negthinspace{\overline{P}}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace=\negthinspace\overline{\Phi}^{i}_{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace+\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[\int_{{\overline{T}}\land{\overline{\gamma}}}^{\overline{T}}g_{i}(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r})dr\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\Big]({\overline{\omega}})

and similarly that

E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\int_{t}^{\overline{T}}\negthinspace h_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\overline{\Psi}^{i}_{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace+\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[\int_{{\overline{T}}\land{\overline{\gamma}}}^{\overline{T}}h_{i}(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r})dr\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\Big]({\overline{\omega}})
.
Since
{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*},\overline{\Phi}^{i}_{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}
and
since
{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{\widehat{\tau}({\overline{W}})\geq{\overline{\gamma}}\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}\geq{\overline{\gamma}}\}}
,
{\overline{P}}-
a.s. by (
6.33
),
we can deduce from the tower property that

\displaystyle\hskip-42.67912pt\int_{{\overline{\omega}}\in{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\Big[\int_{t}^{\overline{T}}g_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\Big]{\overline{P}}(d{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\Big(\overline{\Phi}^{i}_{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{{\overline{T}}\land{\overline{\gamma}}}^{\overline{T}}g_{i}(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r})dr\Big)\Big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\Big]\bigg]

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\Big(\int_{t}^{\overline{\gamma}}g_{i}\big(r,\overline{\mathscr{X}}^{t,{\bf x},\overline{\mu}}_{r\land\cdot},\overline{\mu}_{r}\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{\overline{\gamma}}^{\overline{T}}g_{i}(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r})dr\Big)\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\int_{t}^{\overline{T}}\negthinspace g_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\Big]

and thus
E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\int_{t}^{\overline{T}}g_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\big]\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[\int_{t}^{\overline{T}}g_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\big]\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace y_{i}
.
Analogously, we have
E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\int_{t}^{\overline{T}}h_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\big]\\
\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[\int_{t}^{\overline{T}}h_{i}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace z_{i}
. Hence,
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}
belongs to
{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}(y,z)
.

II.g)
Since the mapping
\ddot{\Psi}
is
\sigma\Big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal G}}}^{t}_{\infty}\big)\Big)\Big/\sigma\big(\mathscr{B}({\cal D}_{\overline{{\cal P}}})\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\ddot{P}}(\mathscr{B}({\cal D}_{\overline{{\cal P}}}))\big)-
measurable by Step (II.a.2),
Theorem
4.1
renders that
D^{V}_{\infty}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\cap{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace:{\overline{V}}\big(\ddot{\Psi}({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\infty\big\}
is
\sigma\Big({\cal F}^{{\overline{W}}^{t}}_{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big({\overline{{\cal F}}}^{t}_{\negthinspace\infty}\big)\Big)-

As
E_{\overline{P}}\big[\int_{t}^{\infty}\negthinspace f^{-}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\big]\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty
,
there is a
{\overline{{\cal N}}}_{\negthinspace f}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{\overline{P}}\big(\mathscr{B}({\overline{\Omega}})\big)
such that

\int_{t}^{\infty}\negthinspace f^{-}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{U}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty

for any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{\negthinspace f}
.

Let
\varepsilon\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(0,1)
.
For any
{\overline{\omega}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{*}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal N}}}^{c}_{\negthinspace f}
,
an analogy to (
6.86
), (
6
) and Theorem

imply that

\displaystyle E_{{\overline{Q}}^{\overline{\omega}}_{\varepsilon}}\big[\,{\overline{R}}(t)\big]

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})}\negthinspace f\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{U}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace E_{\overline{{\bf Q}}_{\varepsilon}(\ddot{\Psi}({\overline{\omega}}))}\big[\,{\overline{R}}({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}))\big]

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}})}\negthinspace f\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{U}}_{r}({\overline{\omega}})\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in(D^{V}_{\infty})^{c}\}}\Big({\overline{V}}\big({\overline{\gamma}}({\overline{\omega}}),{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land\cdot}({\overline{\omega}}),\big({\overline{Y}}_{\negthinspace{\overline{P}}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)({\overline{\omega}}),\big({\overline{Z}}_{\overline{P}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)({\overline{\omega}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\varepsilon\Big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\frac{1}{\varepsilon}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{\omega}}\in D^{V}_{\infty}\}}.

Since
{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{{\cal P}}}_{t,{\bf x}}(y,z)
and since
{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}\geq{\overline{\gamma}}\}}
,
{\overline{P}}-
a.s.

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt{\overline{V}}(t,{\bf x},y,z)\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace E_{{\overline{P}}_{\varepsilon}}\negthinspace\big[\,{\overline{R}}(t)\big]\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}^{c}_{*}}{\overline{R}}(t)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal A}}}_{*}}\Big(\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}}\negthinspace f\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{(D^{V}_{\infty})^{c}}\big[\,{\overline{V}}\big({\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land\cdot},{\overline{Y}}_{\negthinspace{\overline{P}}}({\overline{\gamma}}),{\overline{Z}}_{\overline{P}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\varepsilon\big]\negthinspace\negthinspace+\negthinspace\frac{1}{\varepsilon}{\bf 1}_{D^{V}_{\infty}}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}<{\overline{\gamma}}\}}{\overline{R}}(t)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}\geq{\overline{\gamma}}\}}\Big(\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}}\negthinspace f\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{(D^{V}_{\infty})^{c}}\big[\,{\overline{V}}\big({\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land\cdot},{\overline{Y}}_{\negthinspace{\overline{P}}}({\overline{\gamma}}),{\overline{Z}}_{\overline{P}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\varepsilon\big]\negthinspace+\negthinspace\frac{1}{\varepsilon}{\bf 1}_{D^{V}_{\infty}}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip-14.22636pt\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}<{\overline{\gamma}}\}}{\overline{R}}(t)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}\geq{\overline{\gamma}}\}}\Big(\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}}\negthinspace f\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{(D^{V}_{\infty})^{c}}{\overline{V}}\big({\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land\cdot},{\overline{Y}}_{\negthinspace{\overline{P}}}({\overline{\gamma}}),{\overline{Z}}_{\overline{P}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)\Big)\bigg]\negthinspace-\negthinspace\varepsilon\negthinspace+\negthinspace\frac{1}{\varepsilon}{\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{T}}\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace D^{V}_{\infty}\big).\qquad\qquad

(6.87)

To verify (
6.35
), we set
{\overline{{\cal I}}}^{t}_{\overline{P}}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}<{\overline{\gamma}}\}}{\overline{R}}(t)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}\geq{\overline{\gamma}}\}}\Big(\negthinspace\int_{t}^{\overline{\gamma}}\negthinspace f(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r})dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{V}}\big({\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land\cdot},{\overline{Y}}_{\negthinspace{\overline{P}}}({\overline{\gamma}}),{\overline{Z}}_{\overline{P}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)\Big)
.

\bullet
If
{\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{T}}\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\}\cap D^{V}_{\infty}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
, then

{\overline{V}}(t,{\bf x},y,z)\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}<{\overline{\gamma}}\}}{\overline{R}}(t)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}\geq{\overline{\gamma}}\}}\Big(\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}}\negthinspace f\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\overline{V}}\big({\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land\cdot},{\overline{Y}}_{\negthinspace{\overline{P}}}({\overline{\gamma}}),{\overline{Z}}_{\overline{P}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)\Big)\Big]\\
\negthinspace-\negthinspace\varepsilon

holds for any
\varepsilon\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(0,1)
. Letting
\varepsilon\negthinspace\to\negthinspace 0
gives (
6.35
).

\bullet
If
{\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{T}}\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace D^{V}_{\infty}\big)\negthinspace>\negthinspace 0
and
