, then
E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\overline{{\cal I}}}^{t}_{\overline{P}}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace-\infty\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace{\overline{V}}(t,{\bf x},y,z)
,
so (
6.35
) holds automatically.

\bullet
If
{\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{T}}\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\}\cap D^{V}_{\infty}\big)\negthinspace>\negthinspace 0
and
E_{\overline{P}}\big[\big({\overline{{\cal I}}}^{t}_{\overline{P}}\big)^{-}\big]\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty
,
since Remark
3.2
(1) shows that
E_{\overline{P}}\Big[\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}<{\overline{\gamma}}\}}{\overline{R}}(t)\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}\geq{\overline{\gamma}}\}}\Big(\negthinspace\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}}\negthinspace f\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},\\
{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{(D^{V}_{\infty})^{c}}{\overline{V}}\big({\overline{\gamma}},{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{\gamma}}\land\cdot},{\overline{Y}}_{\negthinspace{\overline{P}}}({\overline{\gamma}}),{\overline{Z}}_{\overline{P}}({\overline{\gamma}})\big)\Big)\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{(D^{V}_{\infty})^{c}}{\overline{{\cal I}}}^{t}_{\overline{P}}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{D^{V}_{\infty}}\int_{t}^{{\overline{\gamma}}\land{\overline{T}}}\negthinspace f\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{T}}<{\overline{\gamma}}\}\cap D^{V}_{\infty}}\pi\big({\overline{T}},{\overline{X}}_{{\overline{T}}\land\cdot}\big)\Big]\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[\big({\overline{{\cal I}}}^{t}_{\overline{P}}\big)^{-}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{t}^{\infty}\negthinspace f^{-}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\big]\negthinspace-\negthinspace c_{\pi}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty
,
we can deduce from (
6.87
) that

\displaystyle{\overline{V}}(t,{\bf x},y,z)\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace-E_{\overline{P}}\Big[\big({\overline{{\cal I}}}^{t}_{\overline{P}}\big)^{-}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{t}^{\infty}\negthinspace f^{-}\big(r,{\overline{X}}_{r\land\cdot},{\overline{U}}_{r}\big)dr\Big]\negthinspace+\negthinspace c_{\pi}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\varepsilon\negthinspace+\negthinspace\frac{1}{\varepsilon}{\overline{P}}\big(\{{\overline{T}}\negthinspace\geq\negthinspace{\overline{\gamma}}\}\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace D^{V}_{\infty}\big),\quad\,\forall\,\varepsilon\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(0,1).

Sending
\varepsilon\negthinspace\to\negthinspace 0
yields
{\overline{V}}(t,{\bf x},y,z)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\infty
, so (
6.35
) still holds. This completes the proof of Theorem
5.1
.
\Box

Appendix A
Appendix

Lemma A.1
.

Let
t_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)
. For
i\negthinspace=\negthinspace 1,2
, let
(\Omega_{i},{\cal F}_{i},P_{i})
be a probability space
and let
B^{i}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{B^{i}_{s}\}_{s\in[0,\infty)}
be an
\mathbb{R}^{d}-
valued continuous process on
\Omega
with
B^{i}_{0}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 0}
such that

\mathfrak{B}^{i}_{s}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace B^{i}_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace B^{i}_{t_{0}}
,
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t_{0},\infty)
is a Brownian motion on
(\Omega_{i},{\cal F}_{i},P_{i})
.
Let
\Phi\negthinspace:\Omega_{1}\negthinspace\mapsto\negthinspace\Omega_{2}
be a mapping such that
\mathfrak{B}^{2}_{s}(\Phi(\omega))\negthinspace=\negthinspace\mathfrak{B}^{1}_{s}(\omega)
for any
(s,\omega)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t_{0},\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{1}
, then

(
i
)

\Phi^{-1}\big({\cal F}^{\mathfrak{B}^{2}}_{s}\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\cal F}^{\mathfrak{B}^{1}}_{s}
,
\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t_{0},\infty]
;

(
ii
)

\Phi^{-1}\big(\mathscr{N}_{P_{2}}({\cal F}^{\mathfrak{B}^{2}}_{\infty})\big)\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\mathscr{N}_{P_{1}}({\cal F}^{\mathfrak{B}^{1}}_{\infty})
;

(
iii
)

\Phi^{-1}\big({\cal F}^{\mathfrak{B}^{2},P_{2}}_{s}\big)\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace{\cal F}^{\mathfrak{B}^{1},P_{1}}_{s}
,
\,\forall\,s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t_{0},\infty]

and
(
iv
)

P_{1}\negthinspace\circ\negthinspace\Phi^{-1}(A)\negthinspace=\negthinspace P_{2}(A)
for any
A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{\mathfrak{B}^{2},P_{2}}_{\infty}
.

The proof of Lemma
A.1
is basic. We refer interested readers to the ArXiv version of
[
4
]
for it.
We also recall the following result from
[
4
]
.

Lemma A.2
.

Let
(\Omega,{\cal F},P)
be a probability space and let
\,t\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)
.
Let
B\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{B_{s}\}_{s\in[0,\infty)}
be an
\mathbb{R}^{d}-
valued continuous process on
\Omega
with
B_{0}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 0}
such that

B^{t}_{s}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace B_{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace B_{t}
,
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
is a Brownian motion on
(\Omega,{\cal F},P)
.

(
1
)
For any
[t,\infty]-
valued
{\bf F}^{W^{t},P_{0}}-
stopping time
\widehat{\tau}
on
\Omega_{0}
,

\widehat{\tau}(B)
is an
{\bf F}^{B^{t},P}-
stopping time on
\Omega
.

(
2
)
Let
\Phi\negthinspace:\Omega\negthinspace\mapsto\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
be a mapping such that
W^{t}_{s}(\Phi(\omega))\negthinspace=\negthinspace B^{t}_{s}(\omega)
for any
(s,\omega)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega
.
For any
[t,\infty]-
valued
{\bf F}^{B^{t},P}-
stopping time
\tau
on
\Omega
, there exists a
[t,\infty]-
valued
{\bf F}^{W^{t},P_{0}}-
stopping time
\widehat{\tau}
on
\Omega_{0}
such that
\tau\negthinspace=\negthinspace\widehat{\tau}(\Phi)
,
P-
a.s.

Lemma A.3
.

Let
\mu\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{\mu_{s}\}_{s\in[0,\infty)}
be a
\mathbb{U}-
valued,
{\bf F}^{W,P_{0}}-
predictable process on
\Omega_{0}
with all paths in
\mathbb{J}
.
Then
\mu^{-1}(A)\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\{\omega_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace:\mu_{\cdot}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace A\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{W,P_{0}}_{\infty}
for any
A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{J})
.

Proof:

Let
\varphi\negthinspace\in\negthinspace L^{0}\big((0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{U};\mathbb{R}\big)
.
The
{\bf F}^{W,P_{0}}-
predictability of
\mu
implies that

\nu_{s}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\varphi\big(s,\mu_{s}(\omega_{0})\big)
,
(s,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(0,\infty)\times\Omega_{0}

is also an
{\bf F}^{W,P_{0}}-
predictable process and
\int_{0}^{\infty}\nu_{s}ds\negthinspace=\negthinspace\int_{0}^{\infty}\varphi(s,\mu_{s})ds

is thus
{\cal F}^{W,P_{0}}_{\infty}-
measurable. Then it holds for any
{\cal E}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R})
that

\mu^{-1}\big((I_{\varphi})^{-1}({\cal E})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big\{\omega_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace:I_{\varphi}\big(\mu_{\cdot}(\omega_{0})\big)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal E}\big\}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big\{\omega_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace:\int_{0}^{\infty}\varphi\big(s,\mu_{s}(\omega_{0})\big)ds\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal E}\big\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{W,P_{0}}_{\infty}
,
which together with Lemma
1.3
(1) shows that the sigma-field
\big\{A\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\mathbb{J}\negthinspace:\mu^{-1}(A)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{W,P_{0}}_{\infty}\big\}

includes all generating sets of
\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{J})
and thus contains
\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{J})
.
\Box

Lemma A.4
.

For any
t\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)
and
(\omega_{0},\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{J}
, define

\mathscr{W}^{t}_{\mathfrak{s}}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\omega_{0}(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega_{0}(t)
and
\mathscr{U}^{t}_{\mathfrak{s}}(\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\mathfrak{u}(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})
,

\,\forall\,\mathfrak{s}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)
.
Then
(t,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\mapsto\negthinspace\mathscr{W}^{t}(\omega_{0})
is a continuous mapping from
[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
to
\Omega_{0}

and
(t,\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace\mapsto\negthinspace\mathscr{U}^{t}(\mathfrak{u})
is a continuous mapping from
[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{J}
to
\mathbb{J}
.

Proof: 1)
Let
(t,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
and let
\varepsilon\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(0,1)
.
Set
N\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\lceil 2\negthinspace-\negthinspace\log_{2}\varepsilon\rceil
and
T\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\lceil t\negthinspace+\negthinspace 1\rceil
. Since
\omega_{0}(s)
is uniformly continuous in
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,N\negthinspace+\negthinspace T]
,
there exists
\displaystyle\delta\negthinspace=\negthinspace\delta(t,\omega_{0},\varepsilon)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Big(0,\frac{\varepsilon}{2^{T+3}}\Big)
such that
\displaystyle|\omega_{0}(s_{1})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega_{0}(s_{2})|\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\frac{\varepsilon}{4N}
for any

s_{1},s_{2}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,N\negthinspace+\negthinspace T]
with
|s_{2}\negthinspace-\negthinspace s_{1}|\negthinspace<\negthinspace\delta
.

For any
(t^{\prime},\omega^{\prime}_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
with
|t^{\prime}\negthinspace-\negthinspace t|\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace\rho_{\overset{}{\Omega_{0}}}(\omega_{0},\omega^{\prime}_{0})\negthinspace<\negthinspace\delta
,
we can deduce that

\displaystyle\rho_{\overset{}{\Omega_{0}}}(\mathscr{W}^{t}(\omega_{0}),\mathscr{W}^{t^{\prime}}(\omega^{\prime}_{0})\big)

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\sum_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\Big(2^{-n}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace\underset{\mathfrak{s}\in[0,n]}{\sup}\,\big|\omega_{0}(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega_{0}(t)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega^{\prime}_{0}(t^{\prime}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\negthinspace+\negthinspace\omega^{\prime}_{0}(t^{\prime})\big|\Big)

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\sum^{N}_{n=1}\Big(2^{-n}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace 2\underset{\mathfrak{s}\in[0,n]}{\sup}\,\big|\omega_{0}(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega^{\prime}_{0}(t^{\prime}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\big|\Big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\sum^{\infty}_{n=N+1}2^{-n}

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace 2\sum^{N}_{n=1}\Big(2^{-n}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace\underset{\mathfrak{s}\in[0,n]}{\sup}\,\big|\omega_{0}(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega_{0}(t^{\prime}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\big|\Big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace 2\sum^{N}_{n=1}\Big(2^{-n}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace\underset{\mathfrak{s}\in[0,n]}{\sup}\,\big|\omega_{0}(t^{\prime}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega^{\prime}_{0}(t^{\prime}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s})\big|\Big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace 2^{-N}

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace 2N\underset{\begin{subarray}{c}s_{1},s_{2}\in[0,N+T]\\
|s_{2}-s_{1}|<\delta\end{subarray}}{\sup}\,\big|\omega_{0}(s_{2})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega_{0}(s_{1})\big|\negthinspace+\negthinspace 2\sum^{N}_{n=1}\Big(2^{-n}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace\underset{s\in[0,n+T]}{\sup}\,\big|\omega_{0}(s)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega^{\prime}_{0}(s)\big|\Big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace\varepsilon/4

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace 3\varepsilon/4\negthinspace+\negthinspace 2\sum^{N}_{n=1}2^{T}\Big(2^{-n-T}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace\underset{s\in[0,n+T]}{\sup}\,\big|\omega_{0}(s)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\omega^{\prime}_{0}(s)\big|\Big)\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace 3\varepsilon/4\negthinspace+\negthinspace 2^{1+T}\rho_{\overset{}{\Omega_{0}}}\big(\omega_{0},\omega^{\prime}_{0}\big)\negthinspace<\negthinspace\varepsilon.

So
(t,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\mapsto\negthinspace\mathscr{W}^{t}(\omega_{0})
is a continuous mapping from
[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
to
\Omega_{0}
.

2)
We next discuss the continuity of mapping
(t,\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace\mapsto\negthinspace\mathscr{U}^{t}(\mathfrak{u})
from
[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{J}
to
\mathbb{J}
.

Denote by
\mathfrak{T}[0,\infty)
the Euclidean topology on
[0,\infty)
.
As
\mathfrak{T}_{\sharp}(\mathbb{J})
is generated by the subbase
\big\{\mathfrak{i}^{-1}_{\mathbb{J}}\big(O_{\frac{1}{n}}(\mathfrak{m}_{k},\phi_{j})\big)\big\}_{n,k,j\in\mathbb{N}}
,
it suffices to show that

A_{n,k,j}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{(t,\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{J}\negthinspace:\mathscr{U}^{t}(\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathfrak{i}^{-1}_{\mathbb{J}}\big(O_{\frac{1}{n}}(\mathfrak{m}_{k},\phi_{j})\big)\big\}

belongs to the product topology
\mathfrak{T}[0,\infty)\negthinspace\otimes\negthinspace\mathfrak{T}_{\sharp}(\mathbb{J})
for any
n,k,j\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
.

Fix
n,k,j\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
and set
\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\underset{(s,u)\in[0,\infty)\times\mathbb{U}}{\sup}\,\big|\phi_{j}(s,u)\big|
.
We pick
(t,\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace A_{n,k,j}
and set
\displaystyle\mathfrak{c}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\frac{1}{5e^{t+1}(\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}\negthinspace+\negthinspace 1)}\Big(1/n\negthinspace-\negthinspace\hbox{$\big|\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\mathfrak{s}}\phi_{j}(\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{u}(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s}))d\mathfrak{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\int_{0}^{\infty}\negthinspace\int_{\mathbb{U}}\phi_{j}(t,u)\mathfrak{m}_{k}(dt,du)\big|$}\Big)\negthinspace>\negthinspace 0
.
By the uniform continuity of
\phi_{j}
, there exists
\lambda\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(0,\mathfrak{c})
such that
\big|\phi_{j}(s_{1},u_{1})\negthinspace-\negthinspace\phi_{j}(s_{2},u_{2})\big|\negthinspace<\negthinspace\mathfrak{c}
for any

(s_{1},u_{1}),(s_{2},u_{2})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{U}
with
|s_{1}\negthinspace-\negthinspace s_{2}|\negthinspace\vee\negthinspace\rho_{\overset{}{\mathbb{U}}}(u_{1},u_{2})\negthinspace<\negthinspace\lambda
.
We define another function
\phi^{\lambda}_{j}
of
\widehat{C}_{b}\big([0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{U}\big)
by

\phi^{\lambda}_{j}(s,u)\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big(\big(1\negthinspace+\negthinspace(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t)/\lambda\big)^{+}\negthinspace\land\negthinspace 1\big)\phi_{j}\big((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t)^{+},u\big)
,
\,\forall\,(s,u)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\mathbb{U}
.
Clearly,
{\cal O}_{\lambda}(\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\mathfrak{i}^{-1}_{\mathbb{J}}\big(O_{\lambda}(\mathfrak{i}_{\mathbb{J}}(\mathfrak{u}),\phi^{\lambda}_{j})\big)
is a member of
\mathfrak{T}_{\sharp}(\mathbb{J})
.

Let
(t^{\prime},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big((t\negthinspace-\negthinspace\lambda)^{+},t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\lambda\big)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\cal O}_{\lambda}(\mathfrak{u})
.
Since
\lambda\negthinspace>\negthinspace\big|\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-s}\big[\phi^{\lambda}_{j}(s,\mathfrak{u}(s))\negthinspace-\negthinspace\phi^{\lambda}_{j}(s,\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\big]ds\big|\negthinspace=\negthinspace\Big|\int_{(t-\lambda)^{+}}^{t}e^{-s}\big(1\negthinspace+\negthinspace(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t)/\lambda\big)\big[\phi_{j}(0,\mathfrak{u}(s))\negthinspace-\negthinspace\phi_{j}(0,\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\big]ds\negthinspace+\negthinspace\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{-s}\big[\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t,\mathfrak{u}(s))\negthinspace-\negthinspace\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t,\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\big]ds\Big|
,
one has

\displaystyle{\cal I}_{1}

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace:=\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\Big|\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{-s+t}\big[\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t,\mathfrak{u}(s))\negthinspace-\negthinspace\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t,\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\big]ds\Big|\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace e^{t}\Big|\int_{(t-\lambda)^{+}}^{t}e^{-s}\big(1\negthinspace+\negthinspace(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t)/\lambda\big)\big(\phi_{j}\big(0,\mathfrak{u}(s)\big)\negthinspace-\negthinspace\phi_{j}\big(0,\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s)\big)\big)ds\Big|\negthinspace+\negthinspace e^{t}\lambda

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace e^{t}\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}e^{-(t-\lambda)^{+}}\lambda\negthinspace+\negthinspace e^{t}\lambda\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace e\lambda\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}\negthinspace+\negthinspace e^{t}\lambda.

We can also estimate:

\bullet

{\cal I}_{2}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big|\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{-s+t}\big[\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t,\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\negthinspace-\negthinspace\phi_{j}((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime})^{+},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\big]ds\big|\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace e^{t}\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{-s}\big|\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t,\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\negthinspace-\negthinspace\phi_{j}((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime})^{+},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\big|ds\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\mathfrak{c}
.

\bullet

{\cal I}_{3}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big|\int_{t}^{\infty}\negthinspace e^{-s}(e^{t}\negthinspace-\negthinspace e^{t^{\prime}})\phi_{j}((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime})^{+},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))ds\big|\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}\big|e^{t^{\prime}}\negthinspace-\negthinspace e^{t}\big|e^{-t}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}e^{t\vee t^{\prime}-t}|t\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime}|\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace e\lambda\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}
.

\bullet

\,{\cal I}_{4}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big|\int_{t}^{\infty}\negthinspace e^{-s+t^{\prime}}\phi_{j}((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime})^{+},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))ds\negthinspace-\negthinspace\int_{t^{\prime}}^{\infty}\negthinspace e^{-s+t^{\prime}}\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))ds\big|\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\int_{t\land t^{\prime}}^{t\vee t^{\prime}}\negthinspace e^{-s+t^{\prime}}\big|\phi_{j}((s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime})^{+},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))\big|ds\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}e^{t^{\prime}-t\land t^{\prime}}|t\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime}|\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace e\lambda\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}
.

Putting them together leads to that

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt\Big|\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\mathfrak{s}}\phi_{j}(\mathfrak{s},\mathscr{U}^{t^{\prime}}_{\mathfrak{s}}(\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}))d\mathfrak{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\int_{0}^{\infty}\negthinspace\int_{\mathbb{U}}\phi_{j}(t,u)\mathfrak{m}_{k}(dt,du)\Big|

\displaystyle\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\Big|\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\mathfrak{s}}\phi_{j}(\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(t^{\prime}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s}))d\mathfrak{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\mathfrak{s}}\phi_{j}(\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{u}(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s}))d\mathfrak{s}\Big|\negthinspace+\negthinspace\Big|\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\mathfrak{s}}\phi_{j}(\mathfrak{s},\mathfrak{u}(t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\mathfrak{s}))d\mathfrak{s}\negthinspace-\negthinspace\int_{0}^{\infty}\negthinspace\int_{\mathbb{U}}\phi_{j}(t,u)\mathfrak{m}_{k}(dt,du)\Big|

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace\Big|\int_{t^{\prime}}^{\infty}e^{-s+t^{\prime}}\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t^{\prime},\mathfrak{u}^{\prime}(s))ds\negthinspace-\negthinspace\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{-s+t}\phi_{j}(s\negthinspace-\negthinspace t,\mathfrak{u}(s))ds\Big|\negthinspace+\negthinspace(1/n\negthinspace-\negthinspace 4e^{t+1}(\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}\negthinspace+\negthinspace 1)\mathfrak{c})

\displaystyle\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\sum^{4}_{i=1}{\cal I}_{i}\negthinspace+\negthinspace\big[1/n\negthinspace-\negthinspace 5e^{t+1}(\|\phi_{j}\|_{\infty}\negthinspace+\negthinspace 1)\mathfrak{c}\big]\negthinspace<\negthinspace 1/n.

This shows
\mathscr{U}^{t^{\prime}}(\mathfrak{u}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathfrak{i}^{-1}_{\mathbb{J}}\big(O_{\frac{1}{n}}(\mathfrak{m}_{k},\phi_{j})\big)

and thus
(t,\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\big((t\negthinspace-\negthinspace\lambda)^{+},t\negthinspace+\negthinspace\lambda\big)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\cal O}_{\lambda}(\mathfrak{u})\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace A_{n,k,j}
.
Then
A_{n,k,j}
is an open set of
\mathfrak{T}[0,\infty)\negthinspace\otimes\negthinspace\mathfrak{T}_{\sharp}(\mathbb{J})
, proving the lemma.
\Box

Lemma A.5
.

Given
t\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)
,
let
\mu\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{\mu_{r}\}_{r\in[t,\infty)}
be a
\mathbb{U}-
valued,
{\bf F}^{W^{t}}-
predictable process on
\Omega_{0}
.
For any
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
, there exists
a
\mathbb{U}-
valued,
{\bf F}^{W^{s}}-
predictable process
\big\{\mu^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\big\}_{r\in[s,\infty)}

on
\Omega_{0}
such that

\mu^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\mu_{r}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)
,
\,\forall\,(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
,
where
{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}\negthinspace:{\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{r}({\overline{\omega}}),~\,\forall\,r\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,s]\big\}
.

Proof: 1)

Define
\Lambda\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\Big\{D\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace:
for any
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
there exists
D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{P}^{W^{s}}
such that
{\bf 1}_{\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\big\}},\;\,\forall\,(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\Big\}
.
Clearly,
\emptyset\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
with
D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\emptyset
for any
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
.
Given
D\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
, by taking the complement of each
D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
, we see that
D^{c}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
.
Let
\{D_{n}\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\Lambda
. For any
n\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}

and
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
, there exists
D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{n}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{P}^{W^{s}}
satisfying

{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D_{n}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{n}\big\}}

for any
(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
.
Then for any
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
,
the
{\bf F}^{W^{s}}-
predictable set
\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\cap}D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{n}
satisfies that

\displaystyle{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\cap}D_{n}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\prod}{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D_{n}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\prod}{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{n}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\cap}D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{n}\big\}}\,,

\,\forall\,(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
.
Namely,
\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\cap}D_{n}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
.
Hence,
\Lambda
is a sigma
-
field of
[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
.

2)
We next demonstrate that
\Lambda
contains all generating sets of
the
{\bf F}^{W^{t}}-
predictable sigma
-
field
\mathscr{P}^{W^{t}}
of
[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
:

\displaystyle\{t\}\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A\hbox{ for }A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{t}\quad\hbox{and}\quad(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A\hbox{ for }q\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\mathbb{Q},~A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{q-}.

(2a)
For any
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
and
(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
, one has

{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in\{t\}\times\Omega_{0}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{r=t\}}
and
{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in(t,\infty)\times\Omega_{0}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{r=(t,\infty)\}}
. They show that
\{t\}\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
with
D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{s=t\}}(\{s\}\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0})\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{s>t\}}\emptyset
,
\,\forall\,(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}

and that
(t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
with
D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{s=t\}}((s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0})\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{s>t\}}([s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0})
,
\,\forall\,(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
.
Since
{\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{t}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{t-}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{\emptyset,\Omega_{0}\}
and since
\emptyset\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
, we see that

\{t\}\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
for any
A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{t}
and that
(t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
for any
A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{t-}
.

(2b)
Fix
q\negthinspace\in\negthinspace(t,\infty)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\mathbb{Q}
and set

\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{A\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace:(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda\big\}
.
Clearly,
\emptyset\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}
.
Since it holds for any
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
and
(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}

that
{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in(q,\infty)\times\Omega_{0}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{r\in(q,\infty)\}}
, we obtain that

(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
with
D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{s\leq q\}}((q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0})\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{s>q\}}([s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0})
,
\,\forall\,(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
.
So
\Omega_{0}
belongs to
\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}
.
Given
A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}
, since
(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
and
(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
,
we can deduce that
(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A^{c}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big((q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\big)\negthinspace\cap\negthinspace\big((q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A\big)^{c}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
and thus

A^{c}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}
. If
\{A_{n}\}_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}
, then

(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Big(\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\overset{}{\cup}}A_{n}\Big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\overset{}{\cup}}\big((q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace A_{n}\big)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
, i.e.,

\underset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\overset{}{\cup}}A_{n}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}
. Thus
\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}
is a sigma-field of
\Omega_{0}
.

Let
q^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,q)
and
{\cal E}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
. We show by three cases that

(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace(W^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
:

(i) If
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,q^{\prime})\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
, then
{\cal E}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{\mathfrak{x}\negthinspace-\negthinspace{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}):\,\forall\,\mathfrak{x}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal E}\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
satisfies that

{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in(q,\infty)\times(W^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E})\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{r\in(q,\infty)\}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})-{\overline{W}}^{t}_{s}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\in{\cal E}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in(q,\infty)\times(W^{s}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}})\big\}}
,

\,\forall\,(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
.

(ii) If
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[q^{\prime},\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace({\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E})
,
it holds for any
(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
that

{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in(q,\infty)\times(W^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E})\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{r\in(q,\infty)\}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\in{\cal E}\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{r\in(q,\infty)\}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}}({\overline{\omega}})\in{\cal E}\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{r\in(q,\infty)\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in(q,\infty)\times\Omega_{0}\big\}}
.

(iii) If
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[q^{\prime},\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace({\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E}^{c})
,
it holds for any
(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
that

{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in(q,\infty)\times(W^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E})\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{r\in(q,\infty)\}}{\bf 1}_{\{{\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}}({\overline{\omega}})\in{\cal E}\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in\emptyset\big\}}
.

So
(q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace(W^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\Lambda
with

D^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\{(s,{\overline{\omega}})\in[t,q^{\prime})\times{\overline{\Omega}}\}}\big((q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace(W^{s}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}})\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(s,{\overline{\omega}})\in[q^{\prime},\infty)\times({\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E}^{c})\big\}}\emptyset\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(s,{\overline{\omega}})\in[q^{\prime},q]\times({\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E})\big\}}\big((q,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\big)\negthinspace+\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(s,{\overline{\omega}})\in(q,\infty)\times({\overline{W}}^{t}_{q^{\prime}})^{-1}({\cal E})\big\}}\big([s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\big)

for any
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
. It follows that

{\cal F}^{W^{t}}_{q-}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\sigma\big(W^{t}_{q^{\prime}};q^{\prime}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,q)\big)\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\widehat{\Lambda}_{q}
.
Then

\Lambda
contains all generating sets of
\mathscr{P}^{W^{t}}
and thus includes
\mathscr{P}^{W^{t}}
.

3)

Let
\{\mu_{s}\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
be a general
\mathbb{U}-
valued,
{\bf F}^{W^{t}}-
predictable process on
\Omega_{0}
.

Let
n\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
, we set
a^{n}_{i}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace i2^{-n}
,
\,\forall\,i\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\{0,1,\cdots,1\negthinspace+\negthinspace 2^{n}\}

and
D^{n}_{i}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\big\{(r,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}\negthinspace:\mathscr{I}\big(\mu_{r}(\omega_{0})\big)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[a^{n}_{i},a^{n}_{i+1})\big\}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{P}^{W^{t}}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\Lambda
,
\,\forall\,i\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\{0,1,\cdots,2^{n}\}
. So for
i\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0,1,\cdots,2^{n}
and
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
, there exists
D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{P}^{W^{s}}
satisfying
{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{n}_{i}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\big\}},\;\,\forall\,(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
.

Fix
(s,{\overline{\omega}})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{\Omega}}
. For any
n\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
, define an
{\bf F}^{W^{s}}-
predictable process
\nu^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}
by
\displaystyle\nu^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{r}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\sum_{i=1}^{2^{n}}{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,\omega_{0})\in\widetilde{D}^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\big\}}a^{n}_{i}
,

\,\forall\,(r,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}
,
where
\widetilde{D}^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{1}\negthinspace=\negthinspace D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{1}
and
\widetilde{D}^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\negthinspace:=\negthinspace D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\big\backslash\Big(\underset{j=1}{\overset{i-1}{\cup}}D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{j}\Big)\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathscr{P}^{W^{s}}
for
i\negthinspace=\negthinspace 2,\cdots\negthinspace,2^{n}
.
Then
\underline{\nu}^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\underset{n\to\infty}{\mathop{\underline{\rm lim}}}\nu^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{r}(\omega_{0})
,
\,\forall\,(r,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}

is a
[0,1]-
valued,
{\bf F}^{W^{s}}-
predictable process and

\displaystyle\mu^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}(\omega_{0})\negthinspace:=\negthinspace\mathscr{I}^{-1}\big(\underline{\nu}^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}(\omega_{0})\big){\bf 1}_{\big\{\underline{\nu}^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}(\omega_{0})\in\mathfrak{E}\big\}}\negthinspace+\negthinspace u_{0}{\bf 1}_{\big\{\underline{\nu}^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}(\omega_{0})\notin\mathfrak{E}\big\}},\quad\,\forall\,(r,\omega_{0})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace\Omega_{0}

defines a
\mathbb{U}-
valued,
{\bf F}^{W^{s}}-
predictable process.

Let
(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
and let
n\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
. For any
i\negthinspace=\negthinspace 2,\cdots,2^{n}
,
since
0\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\cap(\underset{j=1}{\overset{i-1}{\cup}}D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{j})\big\}}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace\sum^{i-1}_{j=1}{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\cap D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{j}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\sum^{i-1}_{j=1}{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{n}_{i}\cap D^{n}_{j}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace 0
,
we obtain

{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in\widetilde{D}^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\big\}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{n}_{i}\big\}}
.
It follows that

\nu^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{r}({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\negthinspace=\negthinspace\sum_{i=1}^{2^{n}}{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in\widetilde{D}^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{i}\big\}}a^{n}_{i}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\sum_{i=1}^{2^{n}}{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{n}_{i}\big\}}a^{n}_{i}
,
\,\forall\,n\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{N}
and thus

\mathscr{I}\big(\mu_{r}({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{n\to\infty}{\lim}\negthinspace\mathop{\uparrow}\,\sum_{i=1}^{2^{n}}{\bf 1}_{\big\{(r,{\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime}))\in D^{n}_{i}\big\}}a^{n}_{i}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{n\to\infty}{\lim}\negthinspace\mathop{\uparrow}\,\nu^{s,{\overline{\omega}},n}_{r}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underline{\nu}^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)
.
Then
\mu^{s,{\overline{\omega}}}_{r}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)\negthinspace=\negthinspace\mu_{r}\big({\overline{W}}({\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\big)
,
\,\forall\,(r,{\overline{\omega}}^{\prime})\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[s,\infty)\negthinspace\times\negthinspace{\overline{{\bf W}}}^{t}_{s,{\overline{\omega}}}
.
\Box

Lemma A.6
.

Given
t\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[0,\infty)
and
{\overline{P}}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathfrak{P}\big({\overline{\Omega}}\big)
, let
{\overline{{\cal W}}}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\big\{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s}\big\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
be a
d-
dimensional Brownian motion
with respect to some filtration
\mathfrak{F}_{\cdot}\negthinspace=\negthinspace\{\mathfrak{F}_{s}\}_{s\in[t,\infty)}
on
\big({\overline{\Omega}},\mathscr{B}({\overline{\Omega}}),{\overline{P}}\big)
.
For any
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
, if
{\overline{\xi}}
is a real-valued,
\mathfrak{F}_{s}-
measurable random variable that is
{\overline{P}}-
integrable,
then
E_{\overline{P}}\big[\,{\overline{\xi}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{\infty}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[\,{\overline{\xi}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\big]
,
{\overline{P}}-
a.s.

Proof:
Fix
s\negthinspace\in\negthinspace[t,\infty)
. Let
t\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace t_{1}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\cdots\negthinspace<\negthinspace t_{n}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\negthinspace=\negthinspace s_{0}\negthinspace<\negthinspace s_{1}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\cdots\negthinspace<\negthinspace s_{k}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty

and let
\{{\cal E}_{i}\}^{n}_{i=1}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\{{\cal E}^{\prime}_{j}\}^{k}_{j=1}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
.

Set
\psi_{k}(x)\negthinspace:=\negthinspace 1
,
\,\forall\,x\negthinspace\in\negthinspace\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
For
j\negthinspace=\negthinspace k,\cdots,1
recursively, the Markov property of the Brownian motion
{\overline{{\cal W}}}

shows that there exists another Borel-measurable function
\psi_{j-1}\negthinspace:\mathbb{R}^{d}\negthinspace\mapsto\negthinspace\mathbb{R}
satisfying

E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}\psi_{j}\big({\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}\big)\big|\mathfrak{F}_{s_{j-1}}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\psi_{j-1}\big({\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j-1}}\big)
,
{\overline{P}}-
a.s. Then we can deduce that

\displaystyle\hskip-34.14322ptE_{\overline{P}}\bigg[\underset{j=1}{\overset{k}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}\Big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\bigg]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Bigg[E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[\underset{j=1}{\overset{k}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}\Big|\mathfrak{F}_{s_{k-1}}\bigg]\bigg|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\Bigg]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[\,\underset{j=1}{\overset{k-1}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{k}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{k})}\Big|\mathfrak{F}_{s_{k-1}}\Big]\bigg|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\bigg]

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[\,\underset{j=1}{\overset{k-1}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}\psi_{k-1}\big({\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{k-1}}\big)\Big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\bigg]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[\,\underset{j=1}{\overset{k-2}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{k-1}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{k-1})}\psi_{k-1}\big({\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{k-1}}\big)\Big|\mathfrak{F}_{s_{k-2}}\Big]\bigg|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\bigg]

\displaystyle\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\bigg[\,\underset{j=1}{\overset{k-2}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}\psi_{k-2}\big({\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{k-2}}\big)\Big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\bigg]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\cdots\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[\,{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{1}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{1})}\psi_{1}\big({\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{1}}\big)\big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\psi_{0}\big({\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s}\big),\quad\hbox{${\overline{P}}-$a.s.}

It follows that

E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{\underset{i=1}{\overset{n}{\cap}}{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{t_{i}}^{-1}({\cal E}_{i})}{\bf 1}_{\underset{j=1}{\overset{k}{\cap}}{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}\Big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{i=1}{\overset{n}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{t_{i}}^{-1}({\cal E}_{i})}E_{\overline{P}}\Big[\underset{j=1}{\overset{k}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})}\Big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\underset{i=1}{\overset{n}{\prod}}{\bf 1}_{{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{t_{i}}^{-1}({\cal E}_{i})}\psi_{0}\big({\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s}\big)
,
{\overline{P}}-
a.s.
By Dynkin’s Pi-Lambda Theorem, the Lambda-system

\big\{A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{\infty}\negthinspace:E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}\big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\overline{\beta}
,

{\overline{P}}-
a.s. for some real
-
valued
{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}-
measurable random variable
\overline{\beta}
on
{\overline{\Omega}}
}

contains the Pi-system
\Big\{\Big(\underset{i=1}{\overset{n}{\cap}}{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{t_{i}}^{-1}({\cal E}_{i})\Big)\bigcap\Big(\underset{j=1}{\overset{k}{\cap}}{\overline{{\cal W}}}_{s_{j}}^{-1}({\cal E}^{\prime}_{j})\Big)\negthinspace:t\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace t_{1}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\cdots\negthinspace<\negthinspace t_{n}\negthinspace\leq\negthinspace s\negthinspace=\negthinspace s_{0}\negthinspace<\negthinspace s_{1}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\cdots\negthinspace<\negthinspace s_{k}\negthinspace<\negthinspace\infty,\,\{{\cal E}_{i}\}^{n}_{i=1}\negthinspace\cup\negthinspace\{{\cal E}^{\prime}_{j}\}^{k}_{j=1}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\mathscr{B}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\Big\}

and thus includes
{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{\infty}
.

Let
{\overline{\xi}}
be a real-valued,
\mathfrak{F}_{s}-
measurable random variable that is
{\overline{P}}-
integrable and let
A\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{\infty}
.
There exists a real
-
valued
{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}-
measurable random variable
\overline{\beta}
such that

E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}\big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\overline{\beta}
,
{\overline{P}}-
a.s.
Since it holds for any
{\cal A}_{s}\negthinspace\in\negthinspace{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\negthinspace\subset\negthinspace\mathfrak{F}_{s}
that

E_{\overline{P}}[{\bf 1}_{{\cal A}_{s}}{\bf 1}_{A}]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{{\cal A}_{s}}E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}\big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\big]\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{{\cal A}_{s}}\overline{\beta}\,\big]
,
we see that
E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace\overline{\beta}\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}\big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\big]
,
{\overline{P}}-
a.s. Then the tower property implies that

\displaystyle E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}{\overline{\xi}}\big]

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\overline{\xi}}E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}\big|\mathfrak{F}_{s}\big]\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\overline{\xi}}E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\big]\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[E_{\overline{P}}\big[\,{\overline{\xi}}E_{\overline{P}}[{\bf 1}_{A}|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}]\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\big]\Big]

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace=\negthinspace

\displaystyle\negthinspace\negthinspace\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[E_{\overline{P}}\big[{\bf 1}_{A}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\big]E_{\overline{P}}\big[\,{\overline{\xi}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\big]\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{A}E_{\overline{P}}\big[\,{\overline{\xi}}|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}]\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\Big]\Big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\Big[{\bf 1}_{A}E_{\overline{P}}\big[\,{\overline{\xi}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\big]\Big].

As
A
runs through
{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{\infty}
, we obtain that

E_{\overline{P}}\big[\,{\overline{\xi}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{\infty}\big]\negthinspace=\negthinspace E_{\overline{P}}\big[\,{\overline{\xi}}\big|{\cal F}^{{\overline{{\cal W}}}}_{s}\big]
,
{\overline{P}}-
a.s.
\Box
